Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the waves space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 169 and the convergence rate is O(803/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to waves theory, we decompose the problem into 169 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 803. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 447. QED.
